C0304	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Picking it writes a constraint naming it and the release that shipped the version picked, by [shipped-bundle identity](../../../deferred.md#the-products-release-channel), the way a [fleet entry](../../10-fleet/README.md) names a credential the factory does not hold.
C0305	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	An upgrade installs the new version beside the one named and moves nothing: the record names one version, that version stays installed while the record stands, and the project moves when the owner picks the new version at [_Factory_](../../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md), which writes the pair.
C0306	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Factory lists each project whose system is a shipped one older than the running release's, so a project held back is a row an owner reads and never a migration that hosting performed on them.
C0307	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Moving is a withdrawal and a second record, never an edit under the standing name, and the mover is always the owner: uploading over the one in force writes the pair in the upload, and picking a shipped version writes it in the pick.
C0308	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The second record is a permanent arrival like any other, so the raise above runs against it and the services whose screens depart from the new version get their conformance items.
C0309	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	What is uploaded decides what can ever be checked against it, and a design system spans the three kinds rather than taking one.
C0310	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	As code, tokens and components with a build, it is diffable, and what the diff decides is bounded the way [_What a diff cannot see_](../../07-contracts/05-what-a-diff-cannot-see.md) bounds a contract's: that a screen is built from the system's components, styled from its tokens, and calls no component against its declared interface.
C0311	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	It does not decide that they were used correctly.
C0312	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The rules for using them, the third thing [_What humans do_](../../../what-humans-do.md) says a design system is, are a document however the rest arrived: decided by nobody, enforced by a safeguard (9) putting a human at the gate, a residue stated rather than promised away.
C0313	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	A contrast floor over the colours a render actually resolves, a name on every control, a focus order, a target size are each decidable against a rendered run of the [candidate](../../06-releases/03-what-a-build-is-called-and-when.md), and each rejects at the [Merge to master](../../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/07-merge-to-master.md) gate like any predicate.
C0314	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	stated	That is also the part of an accessibility regulation a constraint can carry as something a machine decides; the rest of such a constraint is a document with the [compliance officer](../../../what-humans-do.md)'s safeguard, like any rule a machine cannot read.
C0315	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	As a document entire, an export or a page of rules, there is no build and no predicate, nothing checks the result, and the safeguard is the whole of the enforcement.
C0316	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Nothing about that is particular to design: no permanent constraint is checked mechanically unless it arrives as something a machine can read, which is why the compliance officer holding (2) over a regulated area holds a safeguard with it.
C0317	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The predicates a design system supplies are part of its content.
C0318	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	One the factory ships with ships them beside its tokens and its components, so picking that system is picking what a run of a candidate is decided against, and a project that picked one is never left with nothing to decide.
C0319	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	An owner's upload carries whatever predicates were uploaded with it, and one that carried none has no enforceable half over the rendered result.
C0320	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The tokens and the components are still diffed where they arrived as code, and everything a render would show is left to the safeguard (9) the document kind already needs.
C0321	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Which of the two a project is at is read off the constraint in force, which names what arrived, rather than inferred from a run that never failed.
C0322	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The predicates come with the conditions they are decided in, which are part of the system's content as the predicates are: each appearance the system supports, light, dark, and high contrast among them, each viewport width, each text scale, and each text direction.
C0323	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	So a run of a candidate renders every declared state of every screen in every declared condition, and a predicate is decided per state per condition, which [_The third outcome_](../../05-environments/04-what-the-candidate-environment-decides/01-the-third-outcome.md) reads.
C0324	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	A system that declares no condition has one, the run's default, and every verdict records the condition it was decided in, so a pass at one width and one appearance is read as that and never as a pass.
C0325	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	What the system contains is a field of that constraint record, derived when the record is written and never edited after: its tokens, its components, each component's declared interface, and the states each component may be in.
C0326	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Intake derives it from the system's build in the same write, the way the [artifact store](../../01-one-pipeline.md) derives a [consumer contract](../../07-contracts/06-what-a-consumer-declares.md) from a consumer's build, and where no extractor can read that build the field records that it could not be derived, the reading a consumer contract's own non-derivation already gets.
C0327	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	A system arriving as a document has no build and so no field, and the diff over it decides nothing, which is what that kind already costs.
C0328	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The predicates read the states each component may be in: a render of a screen state is decided with each component it shows put in each state the field lists for it, hover and focus and disabled and error among them, so the contrast of a focused control is decided and not only of the resting one.
C0329	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The diff has a set to check against, so the provenance of a component, the origin of a value and a call against a declared interface are each a comparison rather than a judgment.
C0330	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	What the diff compares is read off the service's build by the same per-toolchain extractor the transition check at [_Implementation_](../../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/05-implementation/README.md) runs on, and it is bounded as that check is.
C0331	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Each of the three is decided over what the extractor could follow; a component reached through a construct it could not follow makes the screen **could not derive**, which resolves a human to the gate on the terms stated there rather than reading as conforming.
C0332	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	A [safeguard](../../09-gate-policy/02-one-shape-across-all-of-them.md) (9) has an element to name, which is a component of the system in force, the way it names a [contract element](../../07-contracts/01-two-versioned-things.md) — so an owner who distrusts one component puts a human in front of the items whose builds use it rather than in front of a whole area.
C0333	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	A component's identity in the field is its name, so a move that renames one removes it and adds another.
C0334	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	A safeguard naming a component the record in force lacks is read as naming every component of the system until the owner repoints or withdraws it: it widens rather than lapses, the direction a page with no holder already takes, since nothing in the design may remove a human from a gate.
C0335	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Factory lists each such safeguard beside the move that orphaned it.
C0336	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	And departure becomes a query: the diff of a service's build against the field, read over the [current release](../../06-releases/05-the-deploy-record/README.md) of each service in the reach.
C0337	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	That query is what decomposition enumerates the conformance reach with, so a service that authored no [spec version](../../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/02-spec/README.md) is enumerated like any other.
C0338	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	An [adopted service](../03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md)'s screens are in its build whether or not an item ever declared a state machine over them, and the raise yields an item per service whose build departs, carrying what departed.
C0339	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The predicate half is still out of reach there: a screen with no state machine declares no state to render, and what brings one into reach is the conformance item itself, which declares a state machine at Spec like any item with a user interface.
C0340	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	[_Factory_](../../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md) reports that query per project as three numbers: how many services in the reach depart, how many values their builds resolve outside the token set, and how many uses of a component depart from its declared interface.
C0341	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	They are computed at read time over current releases, like the rest of that screen's numbers, and nothing gates on them.
C0342	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The design system's constraint record is its version.
C0343	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	A constraint is never edited, so the record in force names one design system in one state, and a move writes a second record rather than changing the first.
C0344	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The [build](../../05-environments/01-records-and-one-long-lived-branch.md) names that record beside the third-party set it resolved, so a [release](../../06-releases/02-the-release-record.md) names the design system its screens were built from by naming its build.
C0345	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Which design system a shipped screen came from is then a link followed from the release.
C0346	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	A move removes nothing by itself.
C0347	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	So the pair is still one write and still not an edit: the replaced record's content stays installed beside the content the record in force names, a screen built against the replaced one goes on building, and removal is a later event.
C0348	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The replaced content is removed when no release still restorable names it — finishing the migration is necessary for that and not sufficient.
C0349	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	A service's restorable releases are the range the [declarations in force](../../07-contracts/06-what-a-consumer-declares.md) are computed over, together with every release below it that is still some [rollback's target](../../08-operations/03-overlapping-windows.md), which a window closing out of order leaves there.
C0350	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The removal test reads that set over every service in the reach, and the build's own field is what makes the reading a query rather than an estimate.
C0351	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	For a design system the factory ships, an install therefore carries the one in force and every superseded one a restorable release still names; for an upload, the owner's earlier uploads are kept on the same condition.
C0352	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	What stops the outstanding set growing back is that nothing merges against a replaced record.
C0353	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Decomposition enumerates the reach once, at the raise, so an item drafted before the move and merging after it would leave a departure no conformance item covers.
C0354	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	The [merge queue](../../05-environments/03-the-merge-queue.md)'s re-verification is where that is caught, because a re-verification is a new build naming the record in force when it was performed, as the candidate's own build names the one in force when it was made.
C0355	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Where a build names a design-system record at all and the two differ, the candidate fails its own re-verification where its build uses a component or a token the two records' contents differ on, and goes back the way the queue already sends one back, the attempt counted at [_Implementation_](../../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/05-implementation/README.md).
C0356	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	A candidate using nothing that moved is re-verified against the record in force and merges.
C0357	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Its reading is the one the queue gives a dependency that moved under a candidate: the [score](../../04-risk-score/README.md) learns as from a [hold](../../03-gates/04-what-a-gate-may-change.md) and the [per-author prior](../../04-risk-score/01-factors-at-least.md) does not move, a design-system move being no defect of the author's, and the rejection names the replaced record where it would name a moved release.

C0359	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md	unbuilt M8	Where the system is silent a screen may depart from it, and the departure is decided rather than drifted into.

C0442	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	No link breaks, because what is erased is text inside a record and never the record: the report row, its grouping link, its count, and the before-and-after measurement all stand with the words gone.
C0443	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	An owner performs one erasure as one action, and the factory walks the links that exist — the report to its intent, the statement, the artifact versions authored against them, which is the query the intent's source already marks out.
C0444	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	Who had already read them before redaction is now answerable, from the [read event](../../../deferred.md) each such reading appends to the log.
C0445	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	Its writer is [Factory](../../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md), the writer a [safeguard](../../09-gate-policy/02-one-shape-across-all-of-them.md), a [halt](../../09-gate-policy/04-stopping-the-factory.md) and a [legal hold](../../09-gate-policy/03-what-is-not-in-it/03-a-legal-hold.md) already have, so one erasure is one write by one writer and the auditor is shown one record.
C0446	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	The same action writes the matching erasure-list row, and that row lands first, keyed so a step taken again writes nothing — the order [_Tight integration_](../../../what-the-factory-does/01-tight-integration.md) sets for an event that writes more than one record, the copy a restore replays before the read-through it protects.
C0447	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	What destroys the bytes is each target's own writer — the report store for a report, [intake](README.md) for the statement, the [artifact store](../../01-one-pipeline.md) for an artifact version.
C0448	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	Each reads the redactions naming records it writes, on a pass of its own, and destroys the named spans inside its own records — a record one component writes and another reads, so no component writes another's record.
C0449	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	The erasure is effective at the one write regardless, every reader reading the target through its redactions from the moment the redaction exists, so a store whose destruction lags serves nothing meanwhile.
C0450	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	A redaction outlives a restore.
C0451	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	The bytes it destroys stand destroyed only in the store restored from; a backup taken before an erasure still carries the words, and restoring it would put them back unless something outside the backup says otherwise.
C0452	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	That something is an **erasure list**: an append-only record on the host, outside the [recovery unit](../../../what-the-factory-does/04-what-the-factory-does-not-build.md), naming what a redaction removed and, since [_People_](../../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md) maps a key to a name outside the chain, what an erased mapping removed too.
C0453	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	It lives beside the report store and never in the graph, and the report store is its one writer: Factory calls the store to append a row at a redaction and People calls it at a mapping deletion, so the list has one writer with two callers rather than two writers and no seam.
C0454	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	[The record inventory](../../../records.md) lists it with that row, a store two screens reach being what the inventory exists to enumerate whether or not the store is in the graph.

C0456	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	The same action that writes a redaction, or deletes a mapping, writes the matching row here first, the list is never rolled back by a restore, and each store replays the rows naming its own records against whatever a restore brought back before serving that store again.
C0457	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	A row does not outlive what it protects against.
C0458	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	The store retires a row on its own pass once the erasure it records is older than the [backup retention](../../09-gate-policy/03-what-is-not-in-it/02-retention.md) an owner authored, no backup that could still be restored then carrying the words, and that field has no other reader.
C0459	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	Where an owner authored none, rows are kept for the life of the install, which is what the field is there to end.
C0460	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	What a redaction reaches ends at the install's edge, and both halves of that edge are on the record.

C0463	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	stated	What a provider retained is a residue the factory does not reach: recalling anything from one is the owner's agreement with that provider, which is [substrate](../../../what-humans-do.md) the way hosting is.
C0464	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	stated	And nobody, the factory included, can list a person's reports, because the channel carries no identity by design: an access request is answered by that fact, and an erasure request needs what every redaction needs, content pointed at.
C0465	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	stated	Three things stay refused here, each for a reason already on the record.
C0466	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	stated	A subject key — indexing reports by the people their text mentions — would have the factory identify people in free text, building exactly the join the opaque key refuses to be; whoever the words are about is served by the redaction and not by an index.
C0467	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	stated	Screening content before an agent or a screen reads it is a moderation pipeline a one-customer install does not carry: the admission safeguard above is that screen where an owner wants one, an agent takes report text as [content and never instruction](../../01-one-pipeline.md), and the human the Spec floor keeps is the backstop.
C0468	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	stated	And a reporter gets no status and no way to contest a grouping, because each needs the reporter handle the channel refuses — something to reach, and to recognise, after the submitting session is gone.
C0469	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	Acknowledgment is not among them and is not refused: it is the submit result above, rendered while that session is still open, and folding it in with the two would have given up the cheap one to pay for the expensive ones.
C0470	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/02-reports.md	unbuilt M8	So every report is either linked to the intent it was grouped into or counted as ungrouped, and that count is a number on [_Factory_](../../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md).

C0474	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/03-advisories.md	unbuilt M12	An **advisory** is a published statement that a named version range of a third-party package has a vulnerability, and the fourth [detector](README.md) reads them.

C0477	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/03-advisories.md	unbuilt M12	The condition is mechanical and no model reads a word of it: an advisory names a package and a range, a service's [current release](../../06-releases/05-the-deploy-record/README.md) names a build, and the build's resolved set holds that package inside that range.

C0505	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	What arrives is an **intent**: a request from an owner (1), the end-user [_reports_](02-reports.md) a grouper turned into one (4, 5), or something the factory found itself, raised by a **detector**.
C0506	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	unbuilt M1	Four detectors read what runs: the [health monitor](../../08-operations/01-the-health-monitor.md) still running after its [window](../../08-operations/02-the-analysis-window.md) closed, a [consumer](../../07-contracts/01-two-versioned-things.md) breaking in its own error rate, an [_advisory_](03-advisories.md) naming a package version a shipped [build](../../05-environments/01-records-and-one-long-lived-branch.md) resolved, and a [service level objective](../../08-operations/05-service-level-objectives.md) whose error budget is exhausted or being spent at a rate that exhausts it inside its period.

C0510	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	One record, three sources, and one writer — intake writes every intent, and the three sources are three callers of one entrance rather than three components writing the same record.
C0511	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	What it writes at that event is the source, the actor (seam 1 of [_Deferred, but not designed out_](../../../deferred.md)), whatever constraint arrived with the request and binds only what is decomposed from it, and, for an intent the factory raised, a link to the evidence that raised it.
C0512	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	A [revert](../../06-releases/06-rollback.md) a human asks for at Ops carries the same link, to the release it undoes.
C0513	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	An intent arrives unrefined, and nothing is judged on the way in, because judging it is what [the interview](../02-the-interview.md) is for.
C0514	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	For an intent a detector raised, intake also writes its [tier](../02-the-interview.md) at arrival, from the constraint or the detector that raised it; for the other two sources it is written later, at the interview's last round, and not here.
C0515	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	It also writes the [project](../../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md) the request came in under, where a source supplies one — the project an owner raised it under, the project of the [service](../03-decomposition/README.md) a grouped report came from, or the project named in a detector's evidence.
C0516	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	That field has one job: it is where [_Decomposition_](../03-decomposition/README.md) places a service the work creates, there being no existing service to reach a project through.
C0517	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	unbuilt M1	It is not a claim about where the work is, because a [contract migration](../../07-contracts/02-no-single-item-may-break-a-contract.md)'s decomposed items may be in two projects, and it is not what scopes the constraints [_The interview_](../02-the-interview.md) reads.
C0518	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	Where no source supplies one the field stays unwritten, and intake fills it once if decomposition has to place a created service and nothing else answers; it is never rewritten, so an approval keeps pointing at what was approved.
C0519	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	unbuilt M1	Putting the project on the intent and not on the item is not a contradiction of [_What an item names_](../03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md): an item names a service and reaches a project through it, and an intent names no service.
C0520	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	unbuilt M1	The intent is what [the graph](../../../what-the-factory-does/01-tight-integration.md) joins on.
C0521	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	unbuilt M1	Every item links back to it, which is why one request producing four items in four services needs no record type of its own — the same point [_Work that spans services_](../../07-contracts/10-work-that-spans-services.md) makes from the other end.
C0522	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	So a detector raises an intent for a condition and not for an observation — before raising one it looks for an intent on the same evidence that has not finished, and attaches to that one instead.
C0523	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/01-intake/README.md	built	The evidence is what keys it: this service, this consumer, this [contract](../../07-contracts/01-two-versioned-things.md), this [release](../../06-releases/02-the-release-record.md), this constraint, this objective's period — and for [_Deprecation_](../../07-contracts/08-deprecation.md)'s raises, the element, two marked elements of one contract being two removals.
C0524	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	The factory runs the interview and decides when it has asked enough.
C0525	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	It asks (3) until it has enough to decompose the intent and author a spec per item whose criteria fit the six patterns [_Spec_](../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/02-spec/03-the-six-patterns.md) sets out — and what ends the questioning is having enough to author, not a human declaring the intent clear.
C0526	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	What ends the interview is one more round, fixed in form: the factory states, in the requester's own terms, what it has understood is wanted and what it will build, and the requester's answer confirms that reading or corrects it.
C0527	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Confirmed, the intent is refined; corrected, the answer attaches like any other and the factory asks again, the correction counting a round like every round.
C0528	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	It is owed on an intent that has a requester, which is an owner's request (1), and on an intent grouped from [_reports_](01-intake/02-reports.md) it reaches whoever holds (3), the reporter being reachable by nothing.
C0529	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	An intent the factory raised has no requester, its evidence is the request, and it takes no such round, nobody being able to say the evidence was misread.
C0530	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	At that round the requester also proposes a **tier**: an ordinal value on a small fixed scale that later orders which item is admitted first.
C0531	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	It takes [the shape every authored value has](../09-gate-policy/02-one-shape-across-all-of-them.md): a value in force and a policy-version copy — so an owner's override is a new version and not a rewrite of what the requester proposed.
C0532	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	An intent the factory raised proposes none, taking its tier instead from [the constraint or detector that raised it](01-intake/README.md).
C0533	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	At the same round the requester confirms the intent's **intended effect**: who the change is for and what they should be able to do, or stop having to do, once it ships, one statement in the requester's words.
C0534	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Intake writes it on the intent in the call that records the confirmation, beside the requirements below.
C0535	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	It is not a requirement: no item answers it and no criterion names it.
C0536	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	It is what the acceptance round below asks against, and it is shown on the intent's timeline at [Work](../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md), so a human at any of the intent's gates reads what the change was meant to do for whom and not only what it was to build.
C0537	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	An intent the factory raised has none, its evidence standing where the statement would.
C0538	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	stated	Reading the effect off the running product is refused.
C0539	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	The factory instruments a service to emit [a closed set of counts and durations](../08-operations/01-the-health-monitor.md) and nothing of a request's content or a person's identity.
C0540	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	So whether the people a change was for reached it, used it, or gave up on it is what the requester says once at the acceptance round, and never a series the factory keeps.
C0541	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	No gate fires on that round and no [score](../04-risk-score/README.md) reads it.
C0542	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	The two places a human could otherwise ratify the factory's reading of a request are score-gated and in the steady state auto-pass: confirming the criteria at [_Spec_](../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/02-spec/README.md) (6), and [UAT](../../what-humans-do.md) at [Merge to master](../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/07-merge-to-master.md) (7).
C0543	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	An intent grouped from [reports](01-intake/02-reports.md) is the exception, its source resolving a human to Spec whatever the number is.
C0544	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	The reading is written as a set and not as prose.
C0545	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	A **requirement** is a record attached to the intent, one per statement of what the factory understood is wanted, and its id is opaque, stable and never reused, minted the way a [criterion](../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/02-spec/03-the-six-patterns.md) id is.
C0546	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Intake writes the set, called by the factory, in the same call that records the confirmation, so a corrected round writes none: the factory states a new reading, and the round that confirms that one writes it.
C0547	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	An intent the factory raised enumerates its requirements from the evidence instead.
C0548	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	The record carries which of three it is: confirmed by the requester, enumerated from evidence and confirmed by nobody, or derived by [decomposition](03-decomposition/README.md) as one item's share of a requirement several items answer.
C0549	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	A requirement takes one of the six sentence patterns [_Spec_](../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/02-spec/03-the-six-patterns.md)'s criteria take, EARS being a form for requirements before it is one for criteria: one statement, one response clause, so two behaviours cannot share a statement and a reading cannot be cut coarser than one response.
C0550	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	The factory writes the reading in that form and the requester confirms it, their own words inside the frame.
C0551	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	A statement fitting no pattern is admitted with a tagged reason and counted, the treatment [_The six patterns_](../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/02-spec/03-the-six-patterns.md) gives a criterion, and [Factory](../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md) reports the share so admitted beside the share of criteria in the unwanted-condition pattern it already reports.
C0552	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	A reopened interview ends at a confirming round like the first, and that round writes the new reading against the one before it.
C0553	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	A statement the new reading restates unchanged keeps its record and its id.
C0554	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Every other requirement of the earlier reading is superseded in the same call, pointing at the requirements that replace it, the pointer a superseded [item](03-decomposition/README.md) already carries.
C0555	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	The pointer is a list, since one statement may become two, and empty where the requester retracted it.
C0556	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	The reading in force is the set the last confirming round wrote, and [_Decomposition_](03-decomposition/README.md)'s completeness check reads that set and no earlier one, so a re-decomposition is never asked to answer a statement the requester withdrew.
C0557	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	An item stands through the re-decomposition only where every requirement it answers is still in force; one answering a superseded requirement is superseded with it, which is what keeps [_Spec_](../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/02-spec/03-the-six-patterns.md)'s per-item check reading a set in force without a rule of its own.
C0558	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	A merged item is out of reach, so a criterion in force for a release naming a superseded requirement stays as it is: it names what that release answered, and the completeness query reads through the pointer to what replaced it.
C0559	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Every acceptance criterion names the requirement it answers, so which parts of a request no criterion answers is a query rather than a reconstruction, and [_Decomposition_](03-decomposition/README.md) and [_Spec_](../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/02-spec/03-the-six-patterns.md) are where that query is a rejection.
C0560	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	The interview is not a stage of its own.
C0561	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	It is the state an intent is in, which is why it has no gate and no artifact a gate decides over: intake writes the intent, the interview refines it, decomposition turns it into items.
C0562	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	So the state is a field on the intent, and it has six values.
C0563	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	An intent arrives **unrefined**.
C0564	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	It is **refined** when the factory has enough to decompose it and author a spec per item, which is the same thing as ready to decompose and needs no second name.
C0565	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	It is **re-decomposing** while a re-decomposition of a set in progress is being decided, which is what stops that intent's items while the [Decomposition](../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/01-decomposition.md) firing is open.
C0566	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	It is **escalated** where the rounds exceeded [_the attempt limit_](../03-gates/05-the-attempt-limit.md), or where [decomposition](03-decomposition/README.md)'s re-decompositions did.
C0567	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	That value is written rather than recomputed from either count: the limit is authored with gate policy (8) and changes, and a decision read back against a value that was not in force when it was taken is not the decision that was taken.
C0568	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	It is **dropped** where a human ends it for good, the value an [item](03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md) can already take and for the same reason: some work stops short of anything its items could show, and an end read from absence reads as nothing at all.
C0569	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	And it is **delivered** where the person who asked confirmed that what shipped is what they asked for, which the acceptance round below is.
C0570	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	The state is the intent's own and runs past decomposition in five cases and no others.
C0571	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Exhausting either of the counts the intent keeps writes **escalated** here, the limit exceeded being the intent's own rather than any stage's.
C0572	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Anything sent back to the intent writes **unrefined** again, which is what reopens the interview: a [rework request](../03-gates/06-going-back-up.md) naming it, a gate's reject naming it, a replacement [constraint](01-intake/01-constraints-and-the-design-system.md)'s raise landing on it on the terms that section states, or a correction at the acceptance round.
C0573	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Anything sent back to decomposition writes **re-decomposing** until that Decomposition firing closes.
C0574	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	The state stops every component that could move an item, and not [dispatch](05-dispatch.md) alone.
C0575	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	Three acts advance an item: dispatch putting an agent on a stage, [the gate component](../03-gates/01-where-a-gate-is-and-what-decides-it.md) firing a row, and [the merge queue](../05-environments/03-the-merge-queue.md) fast-forwarding a candidate.
C0576	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Each reads the state before it acts, and none of the three acts on an item whose intent is unrefined, re-decomposing, escalated, or dropped.
C0577	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	An item stands at one of the three at a time, so what it waits on is one row and not three.
C0578	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	That row is written into the log the way [a wait the factory could not compute at a firing](../03-gates/04-what-a-gate-may-change.md) is, by the component that met the state, counting no attempt and teaching the score nothing, and closed by that component when the state clears.
C0579	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	A candidate whose fast-forward has already happened is not covered and needs none: the commit is on master, so the item is merged and past the point a rework request may be raised at all.
C0580	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	A human at [_Work_](../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md) ends one for good, intake writing **dropped** with Work as the caller: an escalation nobody clears, or a conformance intent whose decomposition found nothing departing.
C0581	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	Ending an intent ends its unmerged items with it, Work calling dispatch to write dropped on each in the same action, so nothing of it waits for a second decision or holds a branch.
C0582	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	And the acceptance round writes **delivered** where the person who asked confirms what shipped.
C0583	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	Everything else after decomposition is read from the items.
C0584	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	[An intent partly delivered](04-when-an-intents-items-do-not-all-ship.md) is computed from whether its items all reached a release, and whether an intent was decomposed at all is answered by the items that exist.
C0585	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	So the record keeps one writer: a transition is a write to the intent and goes through intake like every other, and decomposition writes items and calls intake for the one exception that is its own.
C0586	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	One round follows production.
C0587	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	An **acceptance round** is asked once every item of an intent is live: what was asked for, the intended effect the requester confirmed, what shipped, and the releases that carry it, and what it asks is whether the effect was had.
C0588	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	An item is live on the reading [_When an intent's items do not all ship_](04-when-an-intents-items-do-not-all-ship.md) already gives it, a production deploy record naming its release and marking it complete on every production [target](../05-environments/01-records-and-one-long-lived-branch.md).
C0589	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	It is a question record like any other, written by intake and answered at Work, and the [notifier](../08-operations/07-pages.md) delivers it by mail and chat and never a page, nothing being wrong with the software.
C0590	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	It reaches the requester where there is one and whoever holds (3) otherwise, the routing the rounds above already use.
C0591	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Confirmed, the intent is delivered, and the verdict is its [outcome](01-intake/02-reports.md), a verdict on the intended effect and not on the reading.
C0592	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Corrected, the correction attaches as evidence, the intent goes back to unrefined, and the interview reopens the way a replacement constraint's raise reopens one; the round counts against the attempt limit like every round.
C0593	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	An intent the factory raised takes no such round and is delivered when its last item goes live, for the reason its interview takes none: nobody can say the evidence was misread.
C0594	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	No gate fires on the acceptance round and no [score](../04-risk-score/README.md) reads it, for the reason the interview's last round has neither.
C0595	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	The interview reopens where a stage cannot author from what the intent says, which is the ambiguity no amount of re-authoring settles.
C0596	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	A rework request names the intent, or a human at a gate rejects to it, the state goes back to unrefined, and the factory asks — a question record like any other and a round counted against the same limit, so a stage reaching for the intent every time exhausts the rounds and the intent escalates rather than asking forever.
C0597	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	Decomposition then runs again on the answer, superseding only what it actually replaces, because an answer that moves what was asked for moves what the set should have been and leaving the old decomposition standing would ratify it against a request nobody made.
C0598	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Every unmerged item of that intent waits while it is open: the state is already what puts an [agent](../01-one-pipeline.md) on an intent still unrefined, and it is read here to put none on an item whose intent has gone back.
C0599	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	Work shows each of them stopped on the intent's state and not merely as a timeline that is not advancing, which is what an item matching no [fleet entry](../10-fleet/README.md) looks like and is the one thing it must not be confused with.
C0600	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	An intent the factory raised itself has nobody who asked, so its question reaches Work with no [holder](../../what-humans-do.md) and widens to the owner, the way an unheld row does everywhere else.
C0601	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	What is written once is the statement and whatever is attached to it, which is what keeps an approval pointing at what was approved; the state advances.
C0602	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	Nothing reads the state back to justify a decision — it says where the intent is, which is what [_Work_](../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md) answers and what puts an [agent](../01-one-pipeline.md) on the intents still unrefined.
C0603	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	A **question** is a record, one per question asked, attached to the intent and naming the round it was asked in, with the answer written onto it when the answer arrives.
C0604	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Intake writes both, called by the factory at the question and by Work at the answer, because a question is attached to the intent and takes the intent's one writer — the arrangement [_Intake_](01-intake/README.md) already makes for every write to that record, the interview being a state and not a component able to write one.
C0605	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Each round's own spend is on its [agent run record](../10-fleet/01-what-an-agent-runs-on.md) instead, the way every run's is.
C0606	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	It is not an artifact: nothing gates it, no attempt is counted on it, and the spec is still what the refinement produces.
C0607	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	How many rounds have been asked is a field of the intent, written by intake at each round like every other write to it, and it is what [_the attempt limit_](../03-gates/05-the-attempt-limit.md) is compared against here.
C0608	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	Counting the questions instead would count a round that asked three as three, and the limit counts rounds.
C0609	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	Questions appear in [_Work_](../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md) under the intent that raised them, and are answered there.
C0610	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	The spec is the artifact, so there is no interview gate and none is missing: the requester confirms the reading at the last round, and approving the spec confirms the criteria authored from it (6), which is what [_Where a gate is, and what decides it_](../03-gates/01-where-a-gate-is-and-what-decides-it.md) assumes.
C0611	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	An intent whose questions go unanswered does not proceed, and the end condition belonging to the factory is what makes that wait the owner's to end.
C0612	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	An owner who stops answering spends nothing — no build, no environment, no agent is running — so no limit applies to that wait and none should: a limit would turn their silence into an [escalation](../03-gates/05-the-attempt-limit.md) (12) they have to clear anyway, which is the human work the factory exists to remove.
C0613	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	An interview that is answered and still never reaches enough to author does spend, round after round, and it has [_the attempt limit_](../03-gates/05-the-attempt-limit.md) like any other stretch of work the factory can fail at — the same parameter, authored with gate policy (8), counting rounds here rather than retries.
C0614	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	unbuilt M1	Exceeding it appears in Work as an escalation (12), the factory saying it cannot refine this one; it still takes no gate, the spec being the artifact that ratifies the refinement.
C0615	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/02-the-interview.md	built	A human who answers clears the escalation and the count starts again, because the escalation is what put them there and answering it is a decision to spend more — the limit is still the only one the factory holds, and an owner who keeps clearing is choosing to spend past it, limited then by the [spend ceiling](../10-fleet/08-a-spend-ceiling.md) where one is authored on the credential the agent runs on — a stop on units spent through it, restarted by a clearing of its own and not by this one — and by the quota on the [provider account](../10-fleet/01-what-an-agent-runs-on.md) behind it otherwise.
C0616	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	unbuilt M1	Adoption admits one shape: instances on a deploy target, already taking organic traffic, replaceable one at a time and returnable by shifting traffic.
C0617	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	stated	[_What the factory does not build_](../../../what-the-factory-does/04-what-the-factory-does-not-build.md) refuses everything else against that shape, at adoption and at decomposition alike.
C0618	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	unbuilt M1	Most software a customer brings has been running for years, and it is adopted through the same pipeline as anything else: an owner raises an intent (1) naming a repository and the environment it is running in, and the interview asks what it does — the criteria a human can state for behaviour nobody wrote down, which may be none.
C0619	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	unbuilt M1	Decomposition writes the service record for it, the repository already existing, and yields one item, whose implementation stage authors nothing.
C0620	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	unbuilt M1	The repository's head is the implementation, the candidate branch is its [trunk](../../05-environments/01-records-and-one-long-lived-branch.md), and [the build runner](../../05-environments/01-records-and-one-long-lived-branch.md) builds it as it builds any commit.
C0621	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	unbuilt M1	So the item has a [build](../../05-environments/01-records-and-one-long-lived-branch.md), the [consumer contracts](../../07-contracts/06-what-a-consumer-declares.md) the factory derives from one, and the resolved set the [advisory](../01-intake/03-advisories.md) detector reads.
C0622	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	unbuilt M1	Every gate fires as it fires on any item, the [Implementation](../../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/05-implementation/README.md) gate's diff being the whole tree against nothing, and the queue's first fast-forward makes the head master and the item release one.
C0623	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	unbuilt M1	The adoption intent's evidence is the repository named, text the factory did not author that no gate has admitted, so it resolves the source value toward [Spec](../../03-gates/07-what-particular-gates-decide/02-spec/README.md) on the terms [_Factors, at least_](../../04-risk-score/01-factors-at-least.md) sets: a human at the gate whatever the formula returns, once.
C0624	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	unbuilt M1	After the item merges, the repository is on master, admitted content like anything else the factory wrote, so a later item on the adopted service weighs the source rather than resolving it.
C0625	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	unbuilt M1	Its production deploy is where adoption differs from a first release the factory built.

C0633	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/01-a-service-that-already-exists.md	unbuilt M1	The interfaces arrive without [declared meaning](../../07-contracts/05-what-a-diff-cannot-see.md), a consumer's contracts are checked against what was never declared, and naming a field afterwards is a breaking change and a migration's four items.

C0639	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	An area is a record with one writer, an owner declaring one at [_Factory_](../../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md) (9), and its readers are decomposition, dispatch, and every mechanism a safeguard or a scope drawn on one reaches.
C0640	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	Areas form a chain, each naming the area or the project it lies inside, so which of two is narrower is a link rather than a judgment over prose.
C0641	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	Decomposition writes the narrowest area in the chain whose declaration covers the work, and only an area inside the project of the service the item names, so the item's area and its service agree by construction.
C0642	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	A safeguard or a scope drawn on any area in that chain reaches the item: the gate and dispatch follow it from the item's area up to the project.

C0648	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	One more field goes with it, written at the same transitions and kept per stage: how many attempts each stage has taken.
C0649	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	What each stage spent is on the [agent run record](../../10-fleet/01-what-an-agent-runs-on.md) that run wrote, not a field here, and that record is what [_A spend ceiling_](../../10-fleet/08-a-spend-ceiling.md) sums by.
C0650	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	An attempt is counted when a stage is entered to author, so a stage reached once and passed stands at one, and what the events below do is send the item back to be entered again rather than increment anything themselves — which is what makes more than one attempt at a stage mean the work there was redone.
C0651	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	The current stage's count is what a gate compares against [_the attempt limit_](../../03-gates/05-the-attempt-limit.md), because an attempt that failed before a gate fired wrote no decision to count.
C0652	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	It is where an agent's failed attempt, a gate's reject, and [_the merge queue_](../../05-environments/03-the-merge-queue.md)'s rejection all report, each at the stage the item is sent to rather than at the one it leaves, which is what books a [rework request](../../03-gates/06-going-back-up.md) against the stage that was wrong.
C0653	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	unbuilt M2	The stages below a rework request's target re-author counting nothing, a [hold](../../03-gates/04-what-a-gate-may-change.md) reports nothing, and neither does a candidate re-verifying behind someone else's failure.
C0654	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	A human taking an escalated item over (12) clears the escalation and the limit counts from there.

C0656	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	Kept rather than reset at each advance, those counts are what [_Factory_](../../11-screens/01-work-ops-factory-people.md)'s rework rate reads.

C0658	how-the-factory-works/02-intent-into-items/03-decomposition/02-what-an-item-names.md	built	It matches nothing where that role's prompt has no [version in force](../../10-fleet/03-what-an-agent-is-told/README.md), an agent being told one thing.
